### 22.24 W19 VERDICT - the "native scope" question, answered with repo evidence
DECISIVE FINDING (overturns the task book's implicit premise): **the repo's block editor is ALREADY the native one.**
  `BlockView.ets:53` `private static readonly EDITOR_IMPL: string = 'native'` => `:361` renders `BlockEditor({...})`
  = the native `RichEditor` (`BlockEditor.ets:1043`). `BlockView.ets:382`'s `WebEditor` is that constant's `else`
  branch => **unreachable in production** (matches the pre-existing record at `EXEC-PLAN.md:946` verbatim).
  And **E1-E20 ran on exactly this native editor**: E1's title says "B form, block-level native editor"; E11's measured
  criterion is "the `RichEditor` id"; W7's P0 re-verification says "`RichEditor [[W]]Y]]Z`".
  => **"Is a native editor feasible?" is already answered by 20 acceptance tests. It is NOT an open question.**
REMAINING LIVE WebView SURFACE = 4, ALL READ-ONLY RENDERING:
  `MermaidRenderer` (`BlockView.ets:707`) / `MathRenderer` (`:703/:705`) / `CodeBlock` (`:748`) /
  `PdfViewer` (`PdfPage.ets:170` <- `PageView.ets:173`). The whiteboard's shape board is ALREADY fully native
  (`Whiteboard.ets:925` `Canvas` + native gestures; NO `XComponent` anywhere). `PenCanvasPage` has zero references
  (W18 already gave artifact-level proof: `Penkit=0` in `modules.abc`).
EDITOR CONCLUSION: the premise had to be corrected first - **Obsidian itself is Electron** (CodeMirror 6)
  => "web tech implies bad experience" does not hold; the criterion is INTERACTION QUALITY.
  What ArkUI CAN do (SDK surface is sufficient): `getCaretOffset(:2170)` / `getSelection(:2594)`; the span suite
  `addTextSpan(:2390)` / `updateSpanStyle(:2529)`; `decoration(:314)`; real background `textBackgroundStyle(:393)`;
  paragraph indent `leadingMargin(:518)`; `bindSelectionMenu(:2936)`.
  GAP: **no programmatic undo/redo** - only `undoStyle(:3431)`, there is NO `undo()`/`redo()` => a data-level undo
  stack must be built.
  TWO SHORT BOARDS: (1) **large-document performance unproven** (E17 warning / E18 pending / E19 warning / E20 fail
  all deferred to R0 => "Obsidian-level smoothness" is NOT DISPROVEN, NOT PROVEN); (2) **Enter during composition is
  hijacked by `onKeyPreIme`** (trap 21 item 5; W8 A/B'd it negative on 2in1; E15 criterion 4 warning) - **a DEFECT,
  not a capability boundary, and it is fixable**.
  CORRECTION TO THE TASK BOOK (accepted): the "`previewText` not delivered during real composition" evidence came from
  the NATIVE `TextInput` overlay, NOT from WebView => it shows HarmonyOS's app-side IME composition signalling is weak
  overall and **must not be used to argue that a WebView editor feels bad.** (Written into AGENTS as trap 47.)
  A COUNTER-FACT AGAINST "native is always better": the web standard HAS `compositionstart`/`isComposing` while native
  `RichEditor` has no equivalent => on IME composition state, WebView is theoretically STRONGER. This repo's `WebEditor`
  is worse only because its implementation is stale (`isComposing` = 0 hits repo-wide; its page layer uses the
  DEPRECATED `document.execCommand`).
  THE REAL WEBVIEW COST: **W-1 cursor is not reported** - `TextSelectionChangeCallback = (selectionText: string) => void`
  (`web.d.ts:5682`) gives only TEXT, not offsets => the app does not know where the caret is (fatal for an editor).
TIERS: A interaction-dense => must be native (**already all native**); B read-only rendering => WebView acceptable
  (the 4 above; **they carry no IME and no cursors => the W-1 cost does not apply**); C in between (`PdfViewer` selection/annotation).
  **Named "WebView would clearly be worse than competitors"**: **whiteboard** / **block editor** / **search + command palette** (IME composition Enter).
RECOMMENDED SCOPE: **"key capabilities native + content renderers may use WebView"**, structured as
  R1 must be native = editor / whiteboard / all interaction shells;
  R2 whitelist = the 4 read-only renderers (conditions: read-only + no IME + no editable cursor + no competing native gestures);
  R3 forbidden = any EDITABLE text carried by `Web`.
  WHY NOT full-stack native (it refuses to flatter): (1) the payoff is asymmetric - rewriting 3 read-only renderers
  natively means three engines from scratch (thousands of lines / weeks) for a user-perceivable gain of about zero;
  (2) the real risks are all on the NATIVE side (performance red lines + composition Enter + whiteboard ink);
  (3) "using WebView violates Sec.0.0" does not hold - the red line's text is about EXPERIENCE, and Obsidian itself is Electron.
  It also settled W18's open question: the red line means "key capabilities native", NOT "no fallback branches allowed"
  (the latter as a blanket prohibition would be self-contradictory and unenforceable).
IMPACT ON S4: the recommended scope => window count UNCHANGED. Re-judging to full-stack native => net +2 to +4 windows /
  weeks (high uncertainty) and it fixes no confirmed experience defect.
PARALLEL-WINDOW LESSON (new, written into AGENTS as trap 46): with concurrent windows, "git status identical before and
  after" is NOT a valid criterion - W19 entered with ` M module.json5` (not its doing) and exited clean because the
  CONCURRENT W18 reverted it. The criterion must be "was MY OWN write surface empty?".
  W19's own write surface = 2 documents under `Callaite-工作文档/docs/`; ZERO writes inside the repo.
  Note it used Sec.22.23 (not the Sec.22.21 I assigned, which W18 had taken) and wrote to `Callaite-工作文档/docs/`
  rather than a repo-internal `docs/` - correct, since the repo has no `docs/` and AGENTS Sec.0 forbids moving it in.
UNVERIFIED (9 items in its Sec.5): it performed ZERO device operations => every device-level claim is a CITATION, not an
  independent reproduction; simulator != real device (on the tablet, injected keys do NOT go through the IME) => the IME
  conclusion may differ on a real device, and S4-A / criterion 4 were not run; the workload estimates are ESTIMATES
  (no spike record) and must not be used as a budget; "Obsidian-level" has no quantified baseline; `rawfile/spike/` has
  no readings; whether web `isComposing` works on ArkWeb was NOT measured ("web is stronger" is an inference);
  and the whole whiteboard conclusion hangs on W18's single key unknown (does a real device have the Pen Kit HSP?)
  => **W18's real-device smoke test takes priority over this document.**

#### ① `W16-F3` —— ✅ **已闭合：现象 = 「在屏幕下方」，不是缺陷** ✓✓（**本窗口最重要的正面结论**）

**假设**（任务书给的）：`dumpLayout` 只输出**已布局/可见**节点（归档**陷阱 3**）⇒ 「首屏只有一节」很可能是**其余节在下方**。

**判据**：向下滚动 ⇒ **是否出现第 2/3…个日期标题**。**实际用的滚动通道 = `uitest uiInput swipe 1580 1550 1580 460 600`**（**慢速拖拽**；理由见 ⑤：`fling` 会让应用崩溃 ✗）：

| 步 | 视口内日期标题（只统计列表内 `top>=420`） |
|---|---|

⇒ **可复用教训** ✗✓：**「首屏只有 N 个 X」不是「一共只有 N 个 X」** —— 凡用 `dumpLayout` 数**滚动容器内的重复元素**，**必须先证明滚动通道可用** ✓

#### ③ `W13-A` / `W13-B` —— **先取证再决定；两条都不改** ✓

**`W13-A`（缺 `TabState.initialize()`）⇒ Ⓑ 论证「在本仓恒为空操作 ⇒ 无缺陷 ⇒ 不改」** ✓

| # | 事实（逐条可复查） |
|---|---|
| F1 | `ShareReceiveAbility.onCreate` **先 `StatePersistence.load()`**（W13 已修 `:56`） |
| F2 | `load()` 恢复 **`callaite_tabs` + `callaite_activeTabId`**（两键在白名单 `STATE_KEYS` ✓）⇒ **标签组在 `loadContent` 前已存在** |
| F3 | Ⓐ 磁盘真值：`callaite_tabs` = **7 个标签**（含 `2026-10-02`）、`activeTabId` → `2026-10-02` ⇒ **非空** ✓ |
| F4 | `TabState.initialize()` 的**唯一**行为 = ① 空组时建 `Home`；② 否则**把 `currentPage` 对齐到活动标签**（`TabState.ets:65-84` ✓） |
| F5 | `MainContainer.aboutToAppear` **无条件**调 `syncToCurrentPage(currentPage)`（`:169` ✓） |
| F6 | `syncToCurrentPage` **以 `page` 为准**：已有同 `page` 标签 ⇒ 激活；否则**追加**（`:153-168` ✓） |

**推理**：`load()` 后 `tabs` **非空** ⇒ **F4① 不可达**（不可能建 `Home`）✓；分享路径末尾 `setCurrentPage(today)` 而 `today` **恰是活动标签页** ⇒ **`currentPage` 不变** ✓；`aboutToAppear` 时 **F6 找到已有标签 ⇒ 只激活、不追加** ✓；**F4② 即便执行也只把 `currentPage` 对齐到同一个值** ⇒ **值不变** ✓
⇒ ★ **调/不调 `initialize()` 在可观测的 UI 与磁盘上产生同一结果 ⇒ 无差异 ⇒ 无缺陷 ⇒ 不改** ✓（**本会话一贯纪律**：没有差异 ⇒ **不制造没有证据支撑的新语义** ✓）
⚠️ **诚实边界** ✗：这是 **Ⓑ 源码级论证**，**不是 Ⓐ 双构建 A/B**（需改源码 + 两次构建，超出「零改动」约束）⇒ **若要升级为 Ⓐ，另开窗口** ✓
⚠️ **顺带发现（登记，不改）** ✗：`callaite_tabs` 里有一个 **`page:"W"` 的标签，而 vault 里没有 `W.md`**（只有 4 个 `.md` ✓）⇒ **陈旧标签残留**的**活体样本**（与「`activeTabId` 指向已关闭标签」**同族** ✓）；**不成立为缺陷**（打开只会落到空页，不崩 ✓）

#### ⑥ 一条环境事故（**如实登记** ✗）

| 时刻 | 事件 |
|---|---|
| ~22:53 | 应用 a11y 子树消失（`dumpLayout` 返回 **0 应用节点**、pid 仍在）⇒ 按**陷阱 26** 做**设备级重启** ✓ |
| ~22:58 | 重启后应用**启动即崩溃** ✗ —— 新增 **`cppcrash-com.example.callaite-20020050-20261002225818927.log`**（418 969 B）✓ |
| | **根因（读 log 取到）**：`Reason:Signal:SIGABRT(SI_TKILL)` · `LastFatalMessage:[LoadJSPandaFile] load hsp failed, hsp name:com.huawei.hmos.hwstylusfeature/Penkit, errorMsg:Get shared HspPath failed` · 栈 `SourceTextModule::Instantiate` ← `JSNApi::ExecuteModuleBufferSecure` ← `AbilityThread::AttachInner` · **`Process life time: 4s`** ✓ |
| | ⇒ **就是归档陷阱 3**（HSP 在**模块加载期**崩溃，早于任何 `canIUse` 守卫 ✗）—— **本轮活体复现** ✓ |
| ~23:00 | **`emulator.exe -hvd '"MateBook Pro"' -bootMode coldboot`** ⇒ **应用恢复正常**（729 节点、日志流可读）✓ |
| | ⚠️ **归因边界** ✗：「设备级重启导致该 HSP 丢失」**是推断**（**未做「重启前 `bm dump` 该 HSP 存在」的对照**）⇒ **只登记现象与恢复手段** ✓ |

⇒ **与 §22.20（用户红线）的关系** ✓：这条**不是**「建议隔离」，而是**「依赖存在性必须在交付物层面保证」**的**又一条实证** ✓
—— 与 §22.22 的结论（**单 product + 静态 import + 无条件用原生** ✓）**同向** ✓；且**与 §22.22 ⑧-1 的「唯一关键未知」是同一个未知** ✓。

2. ★ **修「截图通道陈旧」的优先级应当上调** ✗✓ —— 它**直接挡死了一整类验收**（任何以**像素/观感**为判据的项，例如 `W13-B` 的主题跟随、验证纪律 8 的浮层开合、R0 批次的「逐像素回基线」✓）⇒ **在它修好之前，那些项在本机一律无法取证** ✓。
3. ★ **清场的正确路线不是「47 次删除」而是「整页删除 + 幂等重建」** ✓（全文 §5.5）—— 但**仍以「删除通道验活」为前置** ✗✓。

### 22.26 W17 VERDICT - four registered gaps settled; zero repo changes
(1) W16-F3 CLOSED: the phenomenon is "BELOW THE FOLD", not a defect. Evidence: a slow `swipe` drag
    (1580,1550 -> 1580,460, 600 ms) brought the 2nd and then the 3rd date heading into view, INCLUDING the
    1037-block day => criterion met => closed. Why W16 was misled (three mechanisms): `dumpLayout` only emits
    LAID-OUT nodes (under virtualisation, off-viewport nodes do not even exist); the first screen's section is itself
    3x the viewport height (today's 48 rows ~ 3456 px vs a 1198 px viewport) so the 2nd heading is necessarily
    ~2250 px below; and W16's scroll channel was unusable that day. REUSABLE CRITERION: before counting repeated
    elements inside a scroll container with `dumpLayout`, first PROVE the scroll channel works. (Written into
    AGENTS as trap 51.)
    SECOND ANSWER: only TWO of the seven days in the window actually have content - 2026-10-02 (48 blocks) and
    2026-09-30 (1037 blocks); 10-01 / 09-29 / 09-28 / 09-27 / 09-26 do not exist on disk at all (each renders one
    empty-state row, an independent reproduction of W16's probe `empty=5`). 2026-09-25 is outside the 7-day window.
(2) W16-F1 NOT closed, and it explicitly refuses to write "unreachable" - the channels were not exhausted.
    Cause: the empty-state row is far down the page and scroll injection repeatedly crashed the app this window,
    with a device-level restart needed each time, while vault bytes had to be preserved => stopped deliberately.
(3) W13-A / W13-B: evidence first, then decide; BOTH UNCHANGED.
    W13-A => a source-level argument that it is a NO-OP in this repo => no defect: `load()` (W13's fix at :56)
    restores `callaite_tabs` + `activeTabId` BEFORE `initialize()`; disk truth shows 7 tabs with a non-empty
    activeTabId; `initialize()`'s only behaviour is (a) build a Home tab when empty, or (b) align currentPage to the
    active tab; `MainContainer.aboutToAppear:169` unconditionally calls `syncToCurrentPage`, which is page-driven
    (activate an existing tab with that page, else append). Since tabs are non-empty and the share path's
    `setCurrentPage(today)` targets the already-active tab, calling or not calling gives the SAME result.
    BOUNDARY: source-level (B), not a two-build A/B (A).
    BONUS REGISTRATION: `callaite_tabs` contains a tab with `page:"W"` while the vault has no `W.md`
    => a live sample of stale-tab residue.
    W13-B => the asymmetry IS real but the A criterion was not obtained: repo-wide there is exactly ONE call site of
    `applyPersistedTheme()` (`EntryAbility.ets:115`) and its own comment says it "must run after AppStorage init and
    before loadContent, otherwise the dark/light resource qualifier will not switch"; the share Ability has its own
    window => B holds. The A criterion needs changing `themeMode="dark"` and starting only the share Ability to see
    whether it is dark - but that criterion is inherently pixel/perceptual and BOTH screenshot channels on this
    machine are stale (see below). Fix defined for when it is verified: one added line, before `windowStage.loadContent`,
    no wrapper.
(4) CLEANUP NOT DONE - the delete channel was not verified (precondition unmet). Channel 1 (`-click <widgetId>`)
    WORKS (used it to enter the journal feed, the settings page and to switch tabs, always "enumerate then click in the
    same round") but could not reach "delete": block rows do not enumerate a delete entry in `-uiLayout -i`, and the
    row-level `...` menu did not appear on either side. Channels 2/3/4 were not attempted. Stop reasons: the
    verification criterion needs all three signals and it could not even construct "disk decreased"; cleanup is
    irreversible (W10's idempotence guard re-inserts a placeholder after a wipe, which would make the before/after byte
    table semantically messy); and this window saw 3 app crashes + 1 a11y-subtree disappearance + 2 device-level
    restarts => W8's lesson applies verbatim.
    VAULT BYTE TABLE: all four pages byte-identical throughout; ZERO shell writes.
THREE NEW TOOL FACTS (written into AGENTS as traps 48-50):
  **W17-C1 (critical)**: BOTH screenshot channels return STALE FRAMES on this machine. `dumpLayout` went
    916 -> 740 -> 466 nodes across three different screens (so it does change), while `snapshot_display`'s md5 stayed
    `E08B04E4...` and `emulator -screenshot`'s md5 stayed `C0607F07...` with a constant 1,388,504 bytes.
    => every acceptance whose criterion is pixel/perceptual CANNOT be settled on this machine.
    => CRITERION: before judging by screenshot, run a positive control on a CHANGED state and require the md5 to differ.
    This complements verification discipline 8 exactly (that one says TEXT SEARCH gives false negatives; this one says
    PIXEL DIFFING also gives false negatives here) => each channel has its own blind spot.
    **=> Fixing the screenshot channel should be PRIORITISED**: it blocks an entire class of acceptance
    (W13-B theme following, discipline 8 overlay open/close, R0's "pixel-identical return to baseline").
  **W17-C2**: `uitest uiInput fling` CRASHES the app (4 calls => pid gone + a new 418,969 B cppcrash log) while
    `swipe` is safe (15 calls, app alive throughout). Crash UNATTRIBUTED (no small-page negative control).
    Also corrects W16: `dircFling`'s actual advance is only 7-40 px per call (direction consistent, magnitude not).
    => scroll channel priority: `swipe` > `dircFling` > `fling` (do not use).
  **W17-C3**: `uitest uiInput`'s coordinate system is the SAME as `dumpLayout`'s (not absolute screen coords):
    clicking the settings row centre (558,1626) with NO offset worked; +515/+518 offsets both failed. This does not
    contradict W13's "dump coords + (518,288) = screen coords" - that conversion is for the physical screen, a
    different purpose.
ENVIRONMENT INCIDENT (registered): the a11y subtree vanished => device-level restart per trap 26 => **the app
  cppcrashed on start**: SIGABRT + `[LoadJSPandaFile] load hsp failed, hsp name:com.huawei.hmos.hwstylusfeature/Penkit,
  errorMsg:Get shared HspPath failed` (stack top `SourceTextModule::Instantiate`, `Process life time: 4s`) => a LIVE
  reproduction of archived trap 3. Recovered with `-bootMode coldboot`.
  *** THIS CONTRADICTS W18's CLAIM *** W18 asserted the local emulator is an OpenHarmony image that "structurally cannot
  contain HMS HSPs" and therefore has zero evidentiary value for the real-device question. But here the HSP WAS present
  (the app had been running normally) and a restart/coldboot made it unavailable. => The emulator DOES carry the Pen Kit
  HSP under normal conditions, and its absence was an emulator-side artifact of the restart. That is GOOD NEWS for the
  real-device question (a HarmonyOS device very likely has it) but it now needs investigation: why did the app try to
  load the HSP at all when W18 proved `Penkit=0` in `modules.abc`? Attribution boundary: "the restart caused the HSP to
  be lost" is an INFERENCE (no pre-restart `bm dump` control).
COMPILE / 7b / REPO: clean build BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 13.7 s; section 7b NOT APPLICABLE (zero source changes, so the
  "is this file in the compile graph" question does not arise - correctly labelled "not applicable", not "done but failed");
  `git status --short` and `-uall` both empty; 0 changed files; 0 probe residue.
UNVERIFIABLE (9): simulator != real device; W16-F1 not verified and its channels not exhausted; W13-B's A criterion not
  obtained; W13-A only B; the `fling` crash unattributed; the Pen Kit HSP loss vs device restart unproven causally;
  W16-F3's headings only counted to 3/7 (the criterion was met so "only 1 section" is disproven, but not exhaustively);
  no whole-screen pixel comparison (this window had only `dumpLayout` as a ruler, so every judgement is node-level);
  section 7b not done.
HANDOVER: device online (MateBook Pro, 2in1, single instance), app alive (pid 2727) on the journal page.
  WARNING: the installed package's `updateTime` (~23:00:13) is EARLIER than this window's final clean-build HAP mtime
  (23:05:58) => the device is still running the 23:00 build, and this window performed ZERO installs
  => must not be written as "the device runs this window's build".
  `preferences` was written BY THE APP ITSELF (md5 drifted CBB5919A... -> e20aec91...) - not by this window.
  vault: four pages byte-identical; 1 new cppcrash (Pen Kit); 0 new jscrash.
THREE ITEMS FOR S4 / THE NEXT WINDOW: (1) move W16-F3 out of "unattributed" - it is not a defect, and it incidentally
  answered "only 2 of the 7 days have content"; (2) PRIORITISE fixing the stale screenshot channel - it blocks a whole
  class of acceptance; (3) the correct cleanup route is "whole-page delete + idempotent rebuild", not 47 individual
  deletes - still gated on verifying the delete channel.

The user confirmed: "AGENTS-归档.md 是我直接删了，我觉得没啥用了" - i.e. the deletion was DELIBERATE, not an
accident. So W22 (the recovery window) was STOPPED, and any file it had regenerated was removed again to restore
the user's intended state.
WHAT W22 DID PROVE BEFORE BEING STOPPED (useful, keep it): the archive W15 produced is EXACTLY reproducible from the
pre-split backup - the only post-W15 delta is a single 13-line W18 fix block inside trap 3 - and the reconstruction
matches W21's recorded 33,240 B / 293 lines exactly. So if the content is ever wanted again, it is recoverable
byte-for-byte from `%TEMP%\AGENTS.md.w15.orig.bak` (68,552 B) using `%TEMP%\w15_split.py`.
WHAT WAS DONE INSTEAD (the only real defect the deletion created): `AGENTS.md` still POINTED at the deleted file,
which is worse than having no pointer at all. Three dangling references were repaired:
  (1) the header pointer line now states that traps 1-25 / 32 / 34 / 35 were DELETED BY THE USER on 2026-10-02 and
      that there is no longer an archive;
  (2) the Sec.7 pointer says the same;
  (3) **trap 33 was re-inlined** (the one archive trap the surviving file still cross-references from trap 36):
      "`preferences/` is shell-writable - the only writable app-data directory besides `graph/`; deleting
      `callaite_app_state` returns the app to onboarding - BUT it is not a fixture-reset mechanism."
  Trap 47's dangling reference to archived trap 21 was redirected to the IME conclusion already stated inline under
  Sec.3's input-channel table.
  Verified afterwards: **0 dangling references remain** in `AGENTS.md` (52,521 B / 538 lines), both red lines
  (Sec.0.0 / Sec.0.1) intact, and all live traps 26-52 present.
NOTE FOR THE FUTURE: the general lesson I wrote at W15 ("a split that loses loading is a loss, not a cleanup") was
  only half-complete - it checked that the ROOT file still loads but not that the ARCHIVE still exists. The complete
  form is: **a split must verify BOTH ends on every future read, or it will silently become a dangling pointer.**
  Here the user's deliberate deletion converted the archive into exactly that.

### 22.38 W0 (N1) - DATA-INTEGRITY WINDOW: both defects REPRODUCED ON DEVICE, N1-a FIXED

Baseline `7112b49` (clean tree). Emulator `MateBook Pro` (2in1 / API 26) started hot with
`emulator.exe -hvd '"MateBook Pro"'`; no `coldboot` needed. Full report:
`docs/S4-W0-N1数据完整性.md`.

**H2 (rename name-collision) - REPRODUCED, all three criteria hold.** (A)
`core/models/Page.ets:92 pageNameToFileName` is many-to-one - a faithful port enumerated
`A/B` `A:B` `A?B` `A*B` `A|B` `A<B` `A>B` `A"B` `A\B` `A_B` ALL -> `A_B.md`; and BOTH
`createPage` (`DataStore.ets:128-136`) and `renamePage` (`:203-229`) have ZERO collision check.
(B) The three rename input boxes have NO character filtering (`LeftSidebar.ets:553-558`,
`PageContextMenu.ets:370-378`, `PageTree.ets:184-189` are bare TextInput + onChange), so the
route IS UI-reachable - this closes the task book's open item Sec.6-3. (C) On device: page P1
(`PAGEA.md`, 30 B, content `- EST`) renamed to `Z`, which was page P2's filename
(`Z.md`, 24 B, no blocks). Disk evidence, verbatim:
  PRE : `PAGEA.md` 30 B md5 `27e98c4a1ebcf3a0638d261e705cb8b4` ; `Z.md` 24 B md5 `0b421b81...`
  POST: `PAGEA.md` -> `No such file or directory` ; `Z.md` 30 B md5 `27e98c4a1ebcf3a0638d261e705cb8b4`
  md count 7 -> 6 ; after `force-stop` + `start` the page list has 6 entries and `PAGEA` is GONE,
  while `Z` now displays PAGEA's content.
=> The POST md5 of `Z.md` EQUALS the PRE md5 of `PAGEA.md` - byte-exact content transfer - while
P2's own 24 B is permanently destroyed. That is the "lost page".

**N1-a (swallowed write failure) - REPRODUCED, `flushed 1/1` observed.** A first probe that threw
BEFORE the funnel (`DataStore.savePage`, above `writeTextSync`) produced `persistNow failed` and
`flushed 0/1` - i.e. the failure WAS reported, which proves ring 3 (`OutlinerEngine.persistNow`)
was always fine and was merely never signalled. The correct probe therefore goes INSIDE
`FileRepository.writeTextSync`'s `try`, before `openSync`, so its fate is identical to a real write
failure (swallowed by the same catch). With it: `[FileRepository] Failed to write file: .../PAGEA.md
Error: W0N1 simulated write failure (ENOSPC-like)` at 19:22:35 x2 and 19:22:51 x1, and
`DirtyPageTracker: flushed 1/1 dirty pages` - while `Z.md` stayed byte-identical (30 B, same md5),
and `[OutlinerEngine] persistNow failed` NEVER appeared (confirming that catch is dead code for
write failures). UI reported success throughout. Probe removed; `git grep W0N1` empty.

**FIX - only N1-a, 2 files.** `writeTextSync` now returns `boolean` (true after `renameSync`,
false in the catch) instead of swallowing everything; `savePage` returns `boolean`, returns early
when the write failed (so it no longer stamps `updatedAt` nor reindexes), and the comment at
`:361-363` that asserted "a failed write throws" - a premise that was simply false - is corrected.
Chosen form is the RETURN VALUE, not a rethrow, because the downstream contract is already boolean
(`persistNow(): boolean`, `saver: (pageId) => boolean`) and `writeTextSync` has exactly ONE call
site repo-wide (`DataStore.ets:347`). With this, `DirtyPageTracker` needs no change at all: its
"re-mark dirty on failure" net becomes reachable for the first time, as does `persistNow`'s catch.
NOT done, deliberately: the temp-file + fsync + rename design is kept (it is the documented last
line of defence), and savePage's other 12 callers are untouched (they ignore the return value).

**H1/H2 FIXES DELIBERATELY DEFERRED.** H1 (rename order: delete-old -> ... -> write-new last) needs
sequencing + rollback and still has an open `待定位` on `fs.renameSync`-over-existing semantics. Now
that N1-a is fixed, an (E)-step failure is at least DISCOVERABLE, which is exactly the split the
task book asked for. H2 needs a COLLISION CHECK, and that CHANGES WHICH PAGE NAMES ARE LEGAL - a
semantic change - so the fix is handed to the user for a ruling; this window delivers reproduction,
options and evidence only.

**REGRESSION.** E1 PASSES on the fixed build: disk 32 B -> 33 B -> 34 B monotonic, content
`- GEONEXY` (both characters landed), `flushed 1/1` x2. W2 silent-block-loss offline criterion:
PASS both cases - my faithful port reproduces the PRE-fix readings exactly (32x `- ` -> 0 blocks /
0 B; 19 empty + `- X` -> 1 block / 3 B) and the POST-fix readings exactly (95 B; 60 B). NOT
executed, therefore NOT claimed as passing: E3, E4, E13, E14, and the S3-W1 30/30 offline probe
(only a code-level argument that its call site is byte-unchanged on the success path).

**HONEST BOUNDARIES.** Simulator, not a real device. N1-a used an injected probe, not a genuine
`ENOSPC` (the task book established no such channel exists; `graph/` remains unwritable from
shell). After the fix only the SUCCESS path was re-tested on device - the fixed FAILURE path is a
code-level argument. A ghost page still appears in the sidebar's 最近 list after a cold start while
全部页面 is correct, which looks like `StatePersistence`'s recent list not reconciling with disk -
logged only, NOT attributed, not fixed. Still open and now explicitly registered: the `AGENTS.md`
correction (`FileRepository` lives in `core/persistence/`, not `core/db/`) was NOT made this
window, because `AGENTS.md` exists as two copies and fixing one would create a fresh inconsistency.

**TWO INSTRUMENT FACTS WORTH KEEPING.** (1) `uitest dumpLayout`'s `id=` and `emulator -uiLayout`'s
`id:` are DISJOINT namespaces (intersection 0 over 116 vs 134 ids), so ids from one channel cannot
be used with the other's click, and both renumber every enumeration - hence the atomic
dump->decide->click driver. (2) `hdc shell cat` converts LF to CRLF, inflating byte counts (30 B
read back as 34 B), so byte-level criteria must use `ls -l` plus `hdc file recv` + local md5.

**VAULT LEFT WITH 3 TEST RESIDUES:** `Z.md` (30 B, H2 fixture), `未命名笔记.md` (34 B, E1 fixture),
and `PAGEA.md` which no longer exists (consumed by the collision). `graph/` is not shell-writable,
so cleanup can only be done in-app; not done this window.
